# Graph Report - KLTN  (2026-09-02)

## Corpus Check
- 768 files · ~1,766,414 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 34487 nodes · 63271 edges · 903 communities (834 shown, 69 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6420 edges (avg confidence: 0.53)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `997fdc9d`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
